// Names for the retail sf33rd.xbe .text. One function per line:
//
// Name = 0x00ADDR; // type:func size:0xNN
//
// size is the matched body, including that function's own int3 padding,
// up to the next function. The scanned span can be longer when MSVC left
// no int3 between a ret and the next function. The C for these names is in src/.
// tools/candidates.py does not write this file.
